Home › Engine functions › other

Engine executable. Settings and tools of the game program (CivilizationVI.exe), usable without a mod. Mostly undocumented debug and configuration functionality; descriptions come from analysis of the executable and have not all been tried in the running game.

other

Everything not matched to a subsystem by its name: template instances, standard-library code, small helpers.

29038 functions in this category, 2771 with a Windows address (2171 high or medium confidence or exported, 600 low). The rest are in the full search.
